# Session 049 morning review inventory

Cleanup and strict second pass are complete. The main sequence of play is readable, but substantial unresolved proper-name spellings, forest religious/history details, and several substantive negotiation/combat sentences remain. These require human review before treating the affected details as reliable. Per the explicit delegation, questions are logged for morning review rather than asked in chat. No human acceptance is recorded. Incidental classifications apply only to contextual fragments whose event/decision is independently explicit nearby, or to unrelated table chatter.

No uncertainty has been accepted. No audio page or clips were requested or created.

- Check the city, scholar, god-caller, forest peoples, tribes, river, gods, and historical names. Repeated phonetic variants are grouped below without selecting spellings.
